Closest to a full reproduction in the corpus: 18 of 20 claims within tolerance; 4-family accuracy exceeds the paper by 2.6 points and the 'plateau is an encoding limit' claim cannot be checked as stated. Paper-scale training uses an exact numpy simulator asserted equal to the Qiskit VQC (exact outcome distributions, shot noise within 1.6 sigma). Findings: the authors' released code compares labels with 'normal.' so the target encoding is constant (reproduces the paper's 86.78% variance; 'as described' mode also run); Qiskit's VQC maps outcome k to class k mod 2 without output_shape, which explains the paper's 9% multiclass result (output_shape=4 gives 0.869 +/- 0.054); the circuit uses reverse_linear entanglement despite the text saying linear. Extensions: depth ablation (reps 1/3/5/9: 0.818/0.880/0.879/0.884) and FakeLimaV2 noisy inference.
